### Dependent NbE (Π/Σ/universes)

- Canonical form: η-long dependent normal forms quoted by type; Π/Σ second components are semantic closure families; cumulative Typeᵢ levels
- Categorification: a categories-with-families (CwF) model: context extension and dependent substitution are closure application
- Decision: bidirectional elaboration plus type-directed conversion; equality is normal-form equality; a term-level strengthening case-splits on discrete Bool context variables, difference-guided by the two normal forms and reporting split nodes/probes (recursive decision after both substitutions, dependent types substituted along; Nat variables are never split) without changing kernel conversion

### Cubical AST and dimension NbE

- Canonical form: separate term/dimension de Bruijn indices in DTerm; line and tube bodies; quoting with dlevel
- Categorification: a cubical CwF with interval contexts; PathP is the dependent path object and Glue values are (raw/DPartial partial, base) pairs
- Decision: bidirectional checking plus Dim/Face simplification and ROBDD semantic predicates

### AST Kan comp/hcomp

- Canonical form: branches are always carried as DSystem; dispatch by type head at a generic dimension; top branches give caps and formal DNComp/DNHComp are the stuck forms
- Categorification: every type former preserves Kan fibration structure; hcompU is Glue gluing
- Decision: system base/overlap compatibility plus recursion on types; circle/torus branch caps are read by substituting 1 into the generic branch value's coordinate expression (keeping a traversed coordinate 1 instead of the endpoint-normalised base 0), so non-point-valued composition in Path and square types computes loop and square caps; coe/fill are segment restrictions of comp

### Persistent environments, laziness and semantic caches

- Canonical form: length-carrying persistent lists DEnv/DDimEnv (innermost de Bruijn head); DVThunk is forced at most once; cache keys are simplified dimensions / quoted-term Debug keys; global DTerm hash-consing builds an intern table bottom-up from subterm stamps so structurally equal subterms share one intern id while rebuilt terms stay structurally equal; the intern table also stores child ids per node, DTermDagSnapshot exports that same DAG as local shells plus child-id edges, and CHDAGBIN v1 imports it by per-node hash-consing with added/reused counts
- Categorification: CwF context extension is cons with a shared tail; lazy values are call-by-need semantic values and memoized families/dimension lines are finite graphs of extensional functions
- Decision: environment indexing, force_count and cache-key equality

### Dependent Kan lines (coe/comp)

- Canonical form: DLine{at, coe, fill} with comp=fill(1); coe goes backwards in Π domains and along the first-component path in Σ

- Decision: recursion on type formers; coe(r,r) is definitionally the identity

### BDD DAGs, budgets and DPLL

- Canonical form: hash-consed DAG node ids; the reachable-node count of a root is its ROBDD size
- Categorification: shared canonical graphs of boolean functions at a fixed variable order; apply is a memoized lattice operation
- Decision: same-manager root-id equality, sat as root≠0, node-budget FaceDecision, or step-budgeted DPLL (the legacy scanner and the watched-literal engine on Tseitin CNF); candidate-order minimization, bounded sifting that moves each variable through every insertion position with an honest initial/final report, and apply-cache doubling rehash once apply_misses exceeds four times the bucket count, and a 3ⁿ worst-case node bound; watched/list DPLL are differentially checked against 2^n brute force; CDCL adds 1-UIP clause learning, VSIDS, Luby restarts, LBD clause deletion (decision level 0 only, reason clauses kept) and RUP/DRUP proof output (deletions are recorded up to literal-set identity and replayed by an independent checker over the evolving active clause database), and is checked on generated faces and pigeonhole instances; provably optimal variable orders come from full permutation enumeration for n≤8 and from an incremental-cofactor subset DP over prefix sets for n≤16, above the cap non-exactness is reported

### Raw DSystem compatibility and first-match normal forms

- Canonical form: compatible systems reuse the type-directed DSystem normal form; arbitrary first-match systems become disjoint effective regions DTubeNF; agreement regions of two values come in both boolean BDD form and a Face-valued form retaining both endpoints, and exact repair truncates branches to the Face-valued agreement without touching endpoints
- Categorification: partial elements in face-relative slices; the checked constructor is a reflection into the composition-system subclass
- Decision: local-context conversion enumerates only the dimensions actually mentioned by the branches (irrelevant dimensions are pruned by BDD cofactor structure), checking the base and pairwise overlaps; the first conflict is reported; exact repair may also sequentially supplement candidates; maximum completion first exact-restricts and then chooses a genuinely maximum compatible supplement as a maximum clique of the branch-compatibility graph, reporting exactness

### First-class AST Equiv and GlueEquiv

- Canonical form: DEquiv carries fwd/bwd/sect/retr terms; DVGlueEquiv carries full equivalence branches and (partial, base) pairs
- Categorification: equivalences in the AST are isomorphisms with homotopy inverse proofs; ua is a functor to universe paths and GlueEquiv glues along systems of partial equivalences
- Decision: bidirectional checking of the four components and two PathP proofs; conversion/quotation recurse over equivalence components and branch faces; coe along ua is definitionally fwd/bwd

### Checked semantic type lines

### Schema registry and elaboration caches

- Canonical form: DInductivePlan/DHitPlan interned in DSchemaRegistry by stable Debug keys, with telescope prefix chains; the CHREGBIN v2 byte form is magic + version + length + checksum + the DAG DTerm/schema codec payload shared with CHMODBIN v3 (v1 remains readable)
- Categorification: schema descriptors are shareable plans; caches store only finite graphs of the same elaboration functions and do not change denotation
- Decision: validation booleans and semantic telescope prefixes are looked up by (term,args,args) keys; hit/miss counters are observable

### Schema functors, positivity and induction-recursion

- Canonical form: the Id/Const/Product/Sum/List/Pi/Container/DecodedFunction/Family grammar of DPositiveFunctor (Family marks host-Self versus external parameter slots with DFunctorParamArg; SelfFamily parameter strict positivity is decided by a least fixed point and foreign nested IHs are transported by term-level functor maps) and its decoded types; DPositivityReport records occurrences and polarities
- Categorification: initial algebras of positive type functors with decoded induction hypotheses; DDecode is the decoding function of a Tarski universe (induction-recursion)
- Decision: polarity recursion on alias-expanded DTerms: nested functors and positive positions are accepted, negative positions rejected, and unknown neutral family applications rejected unless declared; an opaque neutral operator may carry a trusted DPositivityDeclaration (matched under binders by a depth-aware shifting walk, with strict/positive argument positions), and a lambda-verified variant is approved by structural analysis (a declaration is trusted, not a proof of the opaque operator's positivity); IHs are generated by functor recursion

### Schema extensions: mutual induction, quotients and universe polymorphism

- Canonical form: the Sum-indexed merged schema of DMutualFamily; level schemes DLevelExpr; quotients are HITs with equality 1-cells
- Categorification: mutual families are one initial algebra over an index sum; a universe scheme is a function of level assignments; quotients are higher cellular gluing
- Decision: mutual constructors compile with component index constraints and eliminate with Σ motives; schemes evaluate on level vectors and can be revalidated; constructor equations are checked as Path constraints

### Exact univariate polynomials and rational matrices

- Canonical form: dense low-to-high QPoly coefficients with trailing zeros removed; QMat rational row lists; RREF and pivot columns
- Categorification: the Euclidean ring Q[x] and the linear category over Qⁿ; characteristic/minimal polynomials are representation invariants of matrix algebras
- Decision: multiplication is sparse-convolution or common-denominator BigInt integer scaling with schoolbook/Karatsuba kernels and binary exponentiation; primitive pseudo-remainder gcd, Yun squarefree factorization, the rational-root theorem, Faddeev–LeVerrier and RREF; all equality is rational structural equality

### Algebraic extensions and radical normal forms

- Canonical form: QuadNum a+b√d with squarefree d; CubicNum a+b∛m+c∛m² with cubefree m; TriquadNum on the 8-element basis of three squareclass-independent generators; MultiQuadNum on the 16-element basis of up to four generators, aligned by squareclass span; AlgNum remainder polynomials in Q[x]/(m)
- Categorification: quadratic and general finite algebraic extension fields; norm/trace come from the determinant/trace of multiplication matrices
- Decision: residue arithmetic modulo a minimal polynomial and extended-Euclid inversion; multiquadratic inversion by Galois-conjugate products and sign by dyadic interval refinement; radical denesting by square tests; equality is coefficient equality after basis alignment

### Generalized eigenspaces and Jordan theory

- Canonical form: nullity chains of powers of (A−λI), Jordan block sizes and chain bases; PJP⁻¹=A
- Categorification: primary decomposition is a resolution of the identity from coprime polynomial factors; Jordan type is a complete invariant fragment of similarity
- Decision: exact decision from characteristic-polynomial multiplicities, kernel dimensions of powers and chain lifting; non-split cases over Q report residual factors and AlgMat continues over extensions

### Certified numeric shadows and exact cubes

- Canonical form: QuadC=QuadNum+i·QuadNum; CertMat and degree-≤2 CertPath/CertSquare coefficients
- Categorification: exact complex quadratic matrix algebra and polynomial cubes; Double matrices are only approximate representations of exact shadows
- Decision: P²=P, P†=P, orthogonal decompositions and box boundaries are checked by exact structural equality; numeric agreement measures only deviation from shadows and floating residuals

### Spheres and suspension HITs

- Canonical form: north/south points of Susp(X) with one meridian per point; S²=Susp(S¹), S³=Susp(S²)
- Categorification: suspension is the HIT quotient collapsing both ends of X×I to points; iterated suspension gives the sphere family
- Decision: generic HIT schema validation, meridian endpoint face reduction, dependent elimination and hcomp

### Jacobi interval spectral iteration

- Canonical form: RatInterval/CInterval rectangular enclosures and dyadically outward-rounded Hermitian interval matrices; every rotation stores before/after matrices and the accumulated unitary transform
- Categorification: monotone lifting of Hermitian similarity transforms to interval-matrix enclosures; Gershgorin discs give spectral image decompositions and multiplicity clusters
- Decision: exact rational square comparisons certify sqrt/phase/c/s enclosures; every rotation recomputes J†J−I∋0 and J†AJ⊆after, and final discs are clustered and counted; eigenvalue clusters are separated by Sturm-counting the squarefree part (Sturm chain with derivative gcd) and matching multiplicities back by unique interval overlap; the interval iteration itself is cluster-aware: Gershgorin clusters are detected during iteration, a cluster penalty biases pivot choice, precision escalates and reruns when insufficient, and a count-2 cluster is separated into disjoint singletons inside the iteration; an adaptive driver switches to exact characteristic-polynomial separation when the Jacobi envelopes stagnate, retaining exact multiplicities

### Higher-order unification and the bidirectional elaborator

- Canonical form: Miller-pattern spines `?F x₁…xₙ` in UTerm and beta/projection-reduced zonked cores; surface STerm resolves names to de Bruijn UTerm
- Categorification: pattern HOU is the decidable fragment of higher-order unification; bidirectional elaboration sends implicit arguments and dependent motives to the same unifier and compiles to the dependent core
- Decision: rigid decomposition after WHNF, pattern abstraction, occurs/scope checks and a closed-term fallback; a bounded Huet-style search above the deterministic fragment tries rigid/spine decomposition, same-head invariant coordinates, distinct flex-flex constants, imitation and projection transactionally under node/depth budgets and reports solved/no-unifier/residual/budget-exceeded honestly (general HOU is undecidable, so no completeness is claimed); check/infer insert implicit metavariables and unsolved ones are rejected before DTerm translation; phase 11 adds deep imitation, a widened projection-candidate cap and same-head identification, with iterative deepening recorded in HouIterativeReport; phase 12 adds hou_unify_mgu with a three-state semantics on the Miller-pattern fragment — principal MGU, provably no unifier, or a partial substitution together with residual constraints; the phase-13 residual solver alternates principal propagation with transactional iterative-deepening Huet search over the whole residual set (still with no claim of general completeness or a single MGU)

### Parameterized modules and namespaces

- Canonical form: module telescopes (parameters→imports and local declarations interleaved as ModuleItem entries; the legacy decls-only construction still reads, with all imports front-loaded) with separate name→ordinal bindings; instances are simultaneously substituted closed declarations
- Categorification: parameterized modules are module functors, import is inlined functor application, and open/alias/rename are name projections of one telescope entry
- Decision: cycle detection, dependent checking of parameter arguments, ambiguity rejection for import selection/hiding/renaming, neutralization of opaque exports, and dep_check of every closed instance declaration

### Cubical algorithms

- Canonical form: KanBox packages a line, a system and a base; transport fillers are PathP values; path transitivity is the top edge of an hcomp square
- Categorification: path-object operations generated by Kan fibration structure in a cubical CwF: inverse, concatenation, functoriality and dependent substitution
- Decision: dispatch to kernel coe/comp/fill with endpoint conversion; unit-law 3-cells are built by connection rotations of the transitivity square (a two-phase endpoint-preserving rotation on the left, a slide on the right); closed-term builders are accepted again by dep_check_closed

### Computational univalence

- Canonical form: finite universe paths are normalized by wiring equivalences; dependent universe paths are checked type lines, pathToEquiv is their coe equivalence, ua is the Glue line, uaβ unglues a transport filler, and the round-trips are an Equiv homotopy and a 2D Glue square
- Categorification: an equivalence (Equiv A B) ≃ Path U A B between the path category of the universe and equivalences; Glue glues along equivalences and transport is its coe functor
- Decision: wiring equality in the finite layer; coe/comp computation and type-directed conversion of round-trip endpoints and square edges in the AST layer; closed-term pathToEquiv fill/coe proofs are checked by the dependent checker

### Complete exact algebra and root isolation

- Canonical form: BigInt rationals; irreducible factors and multiplicities in Q[x]; Sturm real-root intervals and disjoint Rouché complex disks; split roots in successive algebraic towers; denested radical expressions; biquadratic numbers as rational coefficients on the basis [1,√d₁,√d₂,√(d₁d₂)] for at most two squarefree generators
- Categorification: unique factorization in Q[x], root multisets in an algebraic closure and finite splitting extensions; AlgPoly is polynomial algebra over an extension
- Decision: irreducibility is first decided by a certificate fast path (linear, low-degree without rational roots, mod-p irreducibility, joint exclusion by several mod-p degree patterns, and Eisenstein with shifts — each independently re-verifiable), then factorization prunes by adaptive mod-p degree patterns and rational roots before Kronecker enumeration; Sturm counting, Cauchy-square Taylor exclusion and Rouché subdivision; radical equality is attempted along quadratic, biquadratic, triquadratic, multiquadratic fields with up to four squareclass-independent generators, and the pure cubic Q(∛m); multiquadratic square roots are decided recursively in-field or after adjoining one square-root generator (non-rational square classes and radicands needing general algebraic roots are the stated boundary); every certificate is recomputed with exact polynomial arithmetic

### First-class universe-polymorphic and simultaneous declaration schemas

- Canonical form: level binders and instantiation in DLevelDecl; positive functors extracted after transparent-operator unfolding; iteratively compiled non-uniform nested families; joint mutual and simultaneous IR specifications
- Categorification: universe schemes, parameter-iterated initial-algebra families, joint initial algebras of vector functors and synchronous induction-recursion structures
- Decision: level scope, positivity reports, generated telescopes and dependent conversion; uninstantiated levels are rejected by core checking; beyond decode-position compilation, a mutually dependent simultaneous-IR block whose function level is strictly below the base family level compiles to eliminator closed terms over the single base family (each term verified case by case by forced application in a generic context; nested foreign IHs are covered by functor maps, while forward references, non-universe foreign functions and decoded/level-mismatched positions are still refused explicitly)

### Spherical homotopy representatives and the Hopf invariant

- Canonical form: nested Path types for Ωⁿ; integer degree maps by iterated suspension; the pole-swapped S³ HIT; the Hopf map and phase fibers on the unit sphere in C²
- Categorification: representatives for the suspension functor, based maps and loop spaces; the Hopf S¹-fibration and its linking number
- Decision: conversion checks on generating meridians; IntegerS is a generated inductive type with unique zero/posSucc/negSucc constructor forms and semantic succ/pred maps, and pi3 is presented as Ω³(Sphereₙ) modulo the propositionally truncated Path relation via QuotientS, and the triply iterated suspension unit is rebased through the transitivity-square cancellation by subst; representative loop expressions reduce to integer powers with end-to-end endpoint checks.  This layer is representatives and canonical powers: it does not claim a general synthetic encode/decode proof that π₃(S³)≅ℤ, nor an internal Hopf-invariant proof that π₃(S²)≅ℤ; the unit, inverse and associativity laws and symmetry-of-composite anti-homomorphism are verified face by face on representatives by explicit 3-cells, and every closed representative expression has a recursively assembled normalization 3-cell to the canonical word of its degree (Eckmann–Hilton/interchange are not constructed); beyond floating-point algebraic checks and a Gauss linking integral after stereographic projection numerically deciding |lk|=1, the Hopf landing/fiber-invariance/pole identities are verified in exact rational arithmetic on rational points and rational phase rotations; no internal Hopf map is claimed

### Separate module compilation and linking

- Canonical form: the flattened checked telescope, exports, dependency fingerprints and public interface projection of CompiledModule; abstract declarations have body=None; the cache is indexed by source fingerprints; the stable external form is the CHMODBIN byte array specified in the Decision column (v2 appends a producer string, v3 shares a DAG term codec with CHREGBIN v2); BinaryVersionPolicy carries current/supported/oldest-writable with peer negotiation; CompiledModuleStore keeps module artifact bytes with hit/miss counters; ArtifactRepository layers named blobs, dual-hash manifests, index-last atomic commits, manifest-scan recovery and a CHARTCHN append-only integrity chain (every put/remove links the previous entry digest; the head is an external trust anchor) over an abstract byte store (a protocol, not an OS disk)
- Categorification: compiled objects of module functors and synthetic import-free linking definitions; prefix specialization is partial functor application
- Decision: interface hashes omit opaque bodies and implementation hashes cover all entries; implementations supply abstract exports; cache entries invalidate on source or dependency-interface fingerprints; the stable binary format is magic CHMODBIN + version u16 + length u32 + checksum u32 + payload (tag bytes, fixed-width little-endian i32 fields and UTF-16LE strings), and decoding rejects bad magic/version/truncation/checksum/malformed/trailing bytes before rerunning module validation and fingerprint checks; the loader accepts v1/v2/v3 and migrates v1 with a default producer, while legacy writers refuse newer-only nodes (such as functor tag 8) with None; v3 payloads reference earlier subterms by tag-255 back-references once a node-count threshold is met; corrupted repository blobs are rejected by hash on read, a destroyed index is rebuilt from surviving manifests or by verified chain replay, and edited/reordered/replayed chain entries are rejected against the expected head

### The circle's universal cover and encode/decode

- Canonical form: cover fibres are canonical IntegerS integers over base and ua-line GlueEquiv types over loop points; loop representatives are DVHitCell paths of the schema circle and winding numbers are canonical integer values
- Categorification: the helix is a fibration family from the circle to the universe; encode is the endpoint of substitution (path lifting) in the cover and decode is integer elimination into loop powers
- Decision: the base fibre is convertible to ℤ at universe level; substitution computes directly on the generator, its inverse and refl; a single substitution across the trans points of a composite loop now chains the equivalences through endpoint Glue-line composition and accumulates the winding number correctly (loop²↦2, loop³↦3, loop⁻²↦−2, cancellation↦0, all by conversion), with transport subdivision retained as a comparison path; both round trips are checked by conversion on representatives (a complete internal proof of π₁(S¹)≃ℤ for arbitrary neutral loops remains open)

### Host-opaque cubes and open-box filling

- Canonical form: cubes are host functions (dimension lists to semantic values / rational-polynomial coordinates); an open box is boundary data missing one face and the cap is the filler's restriction to that face
- Categorification: external cubical data over □ with a host implementation of the Kan filling condition; polynomial cubes are cubical maps in exact rational coordinates, filled by the iteration F += w·(S − F|face) face by face
- Decision: boundary completeness and overlap compatibility are verified face by face by typed conversion / canonical polynomial equality; beyond the generic-point probe, stratified extensionality compares host cube functions on finite probe tiers covering face interiors, diagonals (shared/ambient dimensions), connections (¬, ∧, ∨) and axis-specific deep connections, with stratified box validation and verified filling alongside (tiers beyond the probe budget report complete=false); incompatible or incomplete boxes are rejected with failure counts

### Five-member federation bridges

- Canonical form: canonical matrices, graphs, categories, polynomials, finite topologies, homology data and certificates after cross-member conversion
- Categorification: the single federation core obtained by expanding the original three-pillar bridge in place; it connects the five members only on explicit finite common fragments
- Decision: every diagram is rechecked with source-native decision procedures and shared invariants, with direction, side conditions and unsupported fragments recorded

### finalg: number theory and modular arithmetic

- Canonical form: canonical residues, prime-factor tables and CRT solutions
- Categorification: finite fragments of Z/mZ and integer unit groups
- Decision: extended Euclid/Bezout identities, deterministic Miller-Rabin on the 32-bit range and factorization reassembly
